\displaystyle\eta\left(-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}+(1+e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\ln t\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln t\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(\Box)}{=}

\displaystyle\eta\left(-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}+\frac{1}{t}(1+t^{-\mu_{+}}e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+(1+t^{-\mu_{+}}e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle,

where Eq. (
\Box
) is due to
\langle\hat{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle=1
,
\forall\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{S}_{s}
.

Specifically,

\displaystyle-1+(1+t^{-\mu_{+}}e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-1+e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}+t^{-\mu_{+}}e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle t^{-\mu_{+}}e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+(1+t^{-\mu_{+}}e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(t^{-\mu_{+}}e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{(1-\beta)e}\frac{1}{t^{1+\mu_{+}}}e^{-(1+\mu_{+})\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{1+\mu_{+}}}\right).

Case 2:
\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle<0
.
Similar to
Case 1.
, in this case we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\eta\left(\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}+\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\right)\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\left(-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta\left(-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln t\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln t\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln t\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle.

Specifically, if
\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq-t^{-0.5\mu_{-}}
,

\displaystyle\left|\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln t\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+(1-t^{-\mu_{-}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t^{1+0.5\mu_{-}}}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left|-1+\left(1-t^{-\mu_{-}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(\dagger)}{=}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{1+0.5\mu_{-}}}\right),

where Eq. (
\dagger
) is due to if
\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq-t^{-0.5\mu_{-}}
,

\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\left|-1+\left(1-t^{-\mu_{-}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right|=0.

If
-2\leq\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle<-t^{-0.5\mu_{-}}
, we have

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln t\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{1}{t^{\mu_{-}}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{2\mu_{-}}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle.

Therefore, when
t
is large enough,
1-\frac{e^{2\mu_{-}}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}>0
, which by
e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\geq 1-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle
leads to

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{2\mu_{-}}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{2\mu_{-}}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left(1-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\right)\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{2\mu_{-}}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{t^{0.5\mu_{-}}}\right)\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(\frac{1}{t^{0.5\mu_{-}}}+\bm{o}\left(\frac{1}{t^{0.5\mu_{-}}}\right)\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle<0.

If
-2>\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle
,

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln t\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle.

For large enough
t
,
1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}>\frac{1}{2}
, and

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{2}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\frac{e^{2}}{2}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle<0.

Therefore, in
Case 2.
, for large enough
t
, we have

\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\eta\left(\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}+\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\right)\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{1+0.5\mu_{-}}}\right).

Combining
Case 1.
and
Case 2.
, we conclude that

A_{5}(t)\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{1+0.5\mu_{+}}}\right),

which further yields

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}A_{5}(t)<\infty.

(34)

Combining Eq. (
33
) and Eq. (
34
), we conclude that
\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}A_{3}(t)<\infty
, which together with Eq. (
30
) yields
\sum_{t=2}^{\infty}g(t+1)-g(t)<\infty
, and completes the proof.
∎

We are now ready to prove Theorem
2
.

Proof of Theorem
2
.

By Lemma
13
, we have
g(t)
is upper bounded. Therefore, by Lemma
11
, we have
\|\bm{r}(t)\|
is bounded, which further indicates
\|\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}\|
is bounded.

Therefore, the direction of
\bm{w}(t)
can be calculated as

\displaystyle\frac{\bm{w}(t)}{\|\bm{w}(t)\|}=

\displaystyle\frac{\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}}{\|\bm{w}(t)\|}+\frac{\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}}{\|\bm{w}(t)\|}=\frac{\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}}{\|\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}+\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}\|}+\frac{\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}}{\|\bm{w}(t)\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{\bm{w}}}{\left\|\hat{\bm{w}}+\frac{\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}}{\ln t}\right\|}+\frac{\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}}{\|\bm{w}(t)\|}\rightarrow\frac{\hat{\bm{w}}}{\|\hat{\bm{w}}\|}~(as~t\rightarrow\infty).

The proof is completed.

∎

B.2
Implicit regularization of SGDM

This section collects the proof of Theorem
3
. Following the same framework as Appendix
B.1
, we will first prove that the sum of the squared gradient norms along the trajectory is finite. One may expect
{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{\beta}{2\eta}\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\|^{2}
is a Lyapunov function of SGDM. However, due to the randomness of the update rule of SGDM,
{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{\beta}{2\eta}\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\|^{2}
may no longer decrease (we will show this in the end of Appendix
B.2
, please see Appendix
B.2.3
for explanation).

B.2.1
Loss dynamics

Recall that in the main text, we define
\bm{u}(t)
as

\bm{u}(t)=\frac{\bm{w}(t)-\beta\bm{w}(t-1)}{1-\beta},

(35)

where the update of
\bm{u}(t)
is given by
\bm{u}(t+1)=\bm{u}(t)-\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))
.
We then prove Lemma
3
, which indicates
{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))
is a proper choice of Lyapunov function.

Proof of Lemma
3
.

We start the proof by applying the Taylor’s expansion of
{\mathcal{L}}
at the point
\bm{u}(t)
to the point
\bm{u}(t+1)
. Concretely, by Assumption
3
. (S), we have

{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t+1))\leq{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))+\langle\bm{u}(t+1)-\bm{u}(t),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}\|\bm{u}(t+1)-\bm{u}(t)\|^{2},

which by Eq. (
35
) leads to

{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t+1))\leq{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t)),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}\eta^{2}}{2N}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}.

(36)

Taking the expectation of Eq. (
36
) with respect to
\bm{w}(t+1)
conditioning on
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
(recall that
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
is the sub-sigma algebra over the mini-batch sampling, such that
\forall t\in\mathbb{N}
,
\bm{w}(t)
is adapted with respect to the sigma algebra flow
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t+1))|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}]

\displaystyle\overset{(\star)}{=}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\bm{B}(t)}[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t+1))|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\bm{B}(t)}\left[\left.{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t)),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}\right|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(\circ)}{=}

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t)),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}\eta^{2}}{2N}{\mathbb{E}}_{\bm{B}(t)}\left[\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{\overset{(\bullet)}{\leq}}

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t)),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2},

(37)

where Eq.
(\star)
is due to that
\bm{w}(t+1)
is uniquely determined by
\bm{B}(t)
given
\{\bm{w}(s)\}_{s=1}^{t}
, Eq.
(\circ)
is due to
\bm{u}(t)
is uniquely determined by
\{\bm{w}(s)\}_{s=1}^{t}
, and Inequality.
(\bullet)
is due to Lemma
8
.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t+1))|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t)),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t)),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\rangle+\eta\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t)),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))-\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\left(1-\frac{H\eta\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}+\left\langle\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t)),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))-\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\left(1-\frac{H\eta\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\lambda}\left\|\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{2}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))-\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\left(1-\left(\frac{1}{2\lambda}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\eta\right)\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{2}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))-\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\right\|^{2},

where
\lambda
is a positive constant that will be specified latter.

By Assumption
3
. (S),
\ell
is
H
-smooth, which further leads to

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))-\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{H^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{N^{2}}\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{u}(t)\|^{2}\overset{(\square)}{=}\frac{H^{2}\beta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{N^{2}(1-\beta)^{2}}\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{H^{2}\beta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\eta\beta^{t-1-s}\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(\diamond)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{H^{2}\beta^{2}\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{N^{2}}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(\clubsuit)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{H^{2}\beta^{2}\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{N^{2}}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{H^{2}\beta^{2}\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{N^{2}(1-\beta)}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right),

(38)

where Inequality
(\square)
is due to
\beta(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))=(1-\beta)(\bm{u}(t)-\bm{w}(t))
by Eq. (
35
), Inequality
(\diamond)
is due to triangular inequality, and Inequality (
\clubsuit
) is due to Cauchy-Schwartz Inequality.

Combining Eqs. (
37
) and (
38
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t+1))|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\left(1-\left(\frac{1}{2\lambda}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\eta\right)\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}+\frac{\lambda H^{2}\beta^{2}\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2N^{2}(1-\beta)}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right),

which by taking expectation with respect to
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
leads to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t+1))]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\left(1-\left(\frac{1}{2\lambda}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\eta\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\frac{\lambda H^{2}\beta^{2}\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2N^{2}(1-\beta)}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\left(1-\left(\frac{1}{2\lambda}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\eta\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}+\frac{\lambda H^{2}\beta^{2}\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2N^{2}(1-\beta)}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\left(1-\left(\frac{1}{2\lambda}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\eta\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}+\frac{\lambda H^{2}\beta^{2}\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{6}}{2Nb\gamma^{2}(1-\beta)}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right),

where the last inequality is due to
Lemma
8
. Lettting
\lambda=\frac{\sqrt{Nb}\gamma(1-\beta)}{H\beta\sigma_{max}^{3}}
then leads to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t+1))]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(t))-\eta\left(1-\left(\frac{H\beta\sigma_{max}^{3}}{2\sqrt{Nb}\gamma(1-\beta)}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\eta\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}+\frac{H\beta\eta^{2}\sigma_{max}^{3}}{2\sqrt{Nb}\gamma}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\beta^{t-1-s}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right).

By the learning rate upper bound

\eta\leq\frac{1}{\frac{H\beta\sigma_{max}^{3}}{\sqrt{Nb}\gamma(1-\beta)}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}}
,
summing the above inequality over
t
then leads to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(T+1))]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(1))-\eta\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(1-\left(\frac{H\beta\sigma_{max}^{3}}{\sqrt{Nb}\gamma(1-\beta)}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\eta\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(1))-\eta C_{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2},

where
C_{2}\triangleq\left(1-\left(\frac{H\beta\sigma_{max}^{3}}{\sqrt{Nb}\gamma(1-\beta)}+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{4}}{2b\gamma^{2}}\right)\eta\right)
.

The proof is completed.
∎

As
{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(1))
is upper bounded, we have the following corollary given by Lemma
3
.

Corollary 6
.

Let all conditions in Theorem
3
hold. Then, we have

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}<\infty.

(39)

Consequently,

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}<\infty

and

\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle\rightarrow\infty,\forall\bm{x}\in\tilde{\bm{S}}

hold almost surely.

Proof.

By Lemma
3
, we have for any
T>1
,

\sum_{t=1}^{T}C_{2}\eta{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}\leq{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(1))-{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(T+1))]\leq{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{u}(1))<\infty,

which completes the proof of Eq. (
39
). The rest of claims follows immediately by Fubini’s Theorem and Assumption
2
.

The proof is completed.
∎

B.2.2
Parameter dynamics

Similar to the case of GDM, we define
\tilde{\bm{w}}
as the solution of Eq. (
10
) with
{C_{3}}=\frac{\eta}{(1-\beta)N}
. We also let
\bm{n}(t)
be given by Lemma
5
, and define
\bm{r}(t)
in this case as

\bm{r}(t)\overset{\triangle}{=}\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}-\tilde{\bm{w}}-\bm{n}(t).

(40)

As
\tilde{\bm{w}}
is a constant vector, and
\|\bm{n}(t)\|\rightarrow 0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, we have
\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}
has bounded norm if and only if
\|\bm{r}(t)\|
is upper bounded. Similar to the GDM case, we have the following equivalent condition of that
\|\bm{r}(t)\|
is bounded.

Lemma 14
.

Let all conditions in Theorem
3
hold. Then,
\|\bm{r}(t)\|
is bounded almost surely if and only if function
g(t)
is upper bounded almost surely, where
g:\mathbb{Z}^{+}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is defined as

g(t)\overset{\triangle}{=}\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{r}(t)\|^{2}+\frac{\beta}{1-\beta}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\rangle-\frac{\beta}{1-\beta}\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\langle\bm{r}(\tau)-\bm{r}(\tau-1),\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\rangle.

(41)

Proof.

To begin with, we prove that almost surely
|\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\langle\bm{r}(\tau)-\bm{r}(\tau-1),\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\rangle|
is upper bounded for any
t
. By Corollary
6
, we have almost surly

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}<\infty.

On the other hand, for any
\bm{w}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w})\|=\frac{1}{b}\left\|\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{B}(t)}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w},\bm{x}\rangle)\bm{x}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\sigma_{max}}{b}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{B}(t)}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w},\bm{x}\rangle)<-\frac{\sigma_{max}}{b}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w},\bm{x}\rangle)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\sigma_{max}}{b}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w},\bm{x}\rangle)\langle{\hat{\bm{w}}},\bm{x}\rangle\leq\frac{N\sigma_{max}}{b}\left\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w},\bm{x}\rangle)\bm{x}\right\|\|{\hat{\bm{w}}}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{N\sigma_{max}}{b\gamma}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w})\right\|.

Therefore, we have almost surely,

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}<\infty,

which further leads to almost surely

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\|\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\|^{2}\leq\eta^{2}(1-\beta)^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{s=1}^{t}\beta^{t-s}\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta^{2}(1-\beta)^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\beta^{t-s}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta^{2}(1-\beta)^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\beta^{t-s}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\beta^{t-s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(s)}(\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}<\infty.

By the definition of
\bm{r}(t)
(Eq. (
40
)), we further have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\langle\bm{r}(\tau)-\bm{r}(\tau-1),\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}|\langle\bm{r}(\tau)-\bm{r}(\tau-1),\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\rangle|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\left|\left\langle\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)-\ln\left(\frac{\tau+1}{\tau}\right)-(\bm{n}(\tau)-\bm{n}(\tau-1)),\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\|\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\|^{2}+\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\left|\left\langle-\ln\left(\frac{\tau+1}{\tau}\right)-(\bm{n}(\tau)-\bm{n}(\tau-1)),\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\|\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\left\|-\ln\left(\frac{\tau+1}{\tau}\right)-(\bm{n}(\tau)-\bm{n}(\tau-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(\star)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\|\bm{w}(\tau)-\bm{w}(\tau-1)\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\tau=2}^{t}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\right)^{2}<\infty,

where Inequality
(\star)
is due to
\|\bm{n}(\tau)-\bm{n}(\tau-1)\|=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\tau})
and
\ln\frac{\tau+1}{\tau}=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\tau})
.

Therefore,
g(t)
is upper bounded almost surely is equivalent to
\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{r}(t)\|^{2}+\frac{\beta}{1-\beta}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\rangle
is upper bounded, which can be shown to be equivalent with
\|\bm{r}(t)\|
is bounded following the same routine as Lemma
11
.

The proof is completed.
∎

As the case of GDM, we only need to prove
g(t)
is upper bounded to complete the proof of Theorem
3
.

Lemma 15
.

Let all conditions in Theorem
3
hold. Then, for almost every dataset, we have
g(t)
is upper bounded.

Proof.

Following the same routine as Lemma
11
, we have

\displaystyle g(t+1)-g(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{r}(t+1)-\bm{r}(t)\|^{2}+\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{r}(t+1)-\bm{r}(t)\rangle+\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))-(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\rangle,

where
\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\|\bm{r}(t+1)-\bm{r}(t)\|^{2}
is upper bounded.

On the other hand, by the definition of
\bm{r}(t)
(Eq. (
40
)), we have

\displaystyle\bm{r}(t+1)-\bm{r}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)-\ln\left(\frac{t+1}{t}\right)\hat{\bm{w}}-\bm{n}(t+1)+\bm{n}(t),

while by Lemma
5
,

\displaystyle\frac{N}{b}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(t)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\bm{v}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}=\ln\left(\frac{t+1}{t}\right)\hat{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t+1)-\bm{n}(t).

Combining the above two equations, we further have

\bm{r}(t+1)-\bm{r}(t)=\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)-\frac{N}{bt}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(t)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\bm{v}_{i}\bm{x}_{i},

which further indicates

g(t+1)-g(t)=\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{r}(t+1)-\bm{r}(t)\|^{2}+\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))-\frac{N}{bt}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(t)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\bm{v}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle.

Therefore, we only need to prove
\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\langle\bm{r}(t),-\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))-\frac{N}{bt}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(t)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\bm{v}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}\rangle<\infty
. By directly applying the form of
\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))-\frac{N}{bt}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\bm{v}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\frac{\eta}{(1-\beta)b}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\bm{x}_{i}-\frac{N}{bt}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\bm{v}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{(1-\beta)b}\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\bm{x}_{i}-\frac{N(1-\beta)}{\eta}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\bm{v}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{(1-\beta)b}\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\bm{x}_{i}-\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{(1-\beta)b}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\left(-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{(1-\beta)b}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}^{c}}\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle.

Let
A_{6}(t)=\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\left(-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle
, and
A_{7}(t)=\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}^{c}}

\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle
. We will investigate these two terms respectively.

As
\left\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\right\rangle\rightarrow\infty
,
\forall\bm{x}\in\bm{S}
, a.s., we have a.s., there exists a large enough time
t_{0}
, s.t.,
\forall t\geq t_{0}
,
\forall\bm{x}\in\bm{S}
,

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle)\leq(1+e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle},

\displaystyle-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\geq(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle},

\displaystyle\langle\bm{x},\bm{w}(t)\rangle>0.

Therefore,

\displaystyle A_{7}(t)\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}^{c}}-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\mathds{1}_{\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq 0}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}^{c}}(1+e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle})e^{-\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\mathds{1}_{\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq 0}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}^{c}}e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\mathds{1}_{\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq 0}

\displaystyle\overset{(\star)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}^{c}}\frac{1}{t^{\theta}}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\mathds{1}_{\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq 0}

\displaystyle\overset{(\dagger)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{2}{e}\frac{1}{t^{\theta}}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}^{c}}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\mathds{1}_{\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq 0}

\displaystyle\overset{(\circ)}{=}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\theta}}\right),

where Inequality.
(\star)
is due the definition of
\theta
(Eq. (
32
)), Inequality. (
\dagger
) is due to
e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\leq e^{-1}
, and Eq.
(\circ)
is due to
\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}=e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}
. Thus,

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}A_{7}(t)<\infty.

On the other hand,
A_{6}(t)
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle A_{6}(t)=

\displaystyle\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\left(-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\mathds{1}_{\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq 0}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(s)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\left(-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\mathds{1}_{\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle<0}.

If
\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq 0
, we have for
\varepsilon<0.5
,

\displaystyle\left(-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\left(1+e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\left(1+e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\left(1+e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)\frac{1}{t}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\left(1+\frac{1}{t^{\mu_{+}}}e^{-\mu_{+}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)\frac{1}{t}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}

\displaystyle\overset{(\bullet)}{=}

\displaystyle\left(\left(1+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\mu_{+}}}\right)\right)\left(1+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{0.5-\varepsilon}}\right)\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)\frac{1}{t}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}-1\right)\frac{1}{t}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}+\frac{1}{t}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\min\{\mu_{+},0.5-\varepsilon\}}}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\min\{\mu_{+},0.5-\varepsilon\}}}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}

\displaystyle\overset{(\diamond)}{=}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\min\{1+\mu_{+},1.5-\varepsilon\}}}\right),

where Eq.
(\bullet)
is due to
\bm{n}(t)=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{t^{0.5{-}\varepsilon}})
, and Eq. (
\diamond
) is due to
e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\leq\frac{1}{e}
.

On the other hand, if
\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle<0
, we have

\displaystyle\left(-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

Specifically, if
\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq-t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t^{1+0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left|-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(\square)}{=}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{1+0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}}\right),

where Eq. (
\square
) is due to that as
\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\rightarrow\infty
and
t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}\rightarrow 0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
, there exists a large enough time
T
, s.t.,
\forall t>T
, under the circumstance
0>\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\geq-t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}
,
e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}<1
and
e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}<1
.

If
-2\leq\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle<-t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}
, then, for large enough
t
,
|\langle\bm{x}_{i},\bm{n}(t)\rangle|<2
,
1-\frac{e^{{\mu_{-}}(-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle+4)}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}>0
, and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}+\tilde{\bm{w}}+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{-{\mu_{-}}\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{{\mu_{-}}(-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle+4)}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{{\mu_{-}}(-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle+4)}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}\right)\left(1-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\right)\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{{\mu_{-}}(-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle+4)}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}\right)\left(1+t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}-\langle\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\right)\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-\frac{e^{{\mu_{-}}(-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle+4)}}{t^{\mu_{-}}}\right)\left(1+t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}+\bm{o}\left(t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}\right)\right)\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+1+t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}+\bm{o}\left(t^{-0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}\right)\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle<0.

If
-2>\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle
, then for large enough time
t
,
e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\geq e^{\frac{3}{2}}
,
1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\geq e^{-\frac{1}{2}}
, and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+\left(1-e^{-\mu_{-}\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)e^{-\langle\bm{r}(t)+\bm{n}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\left(-1+e\right)\left\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle<0.

Conclusively, if
\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle<0
, for large enough
t
, we have

\left(-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle)-\frac{1}{t}e^{-\langle\tilde{\bm{w}},\bm{x}_{i}\rangle}\right)\langle\bm{r}(t),\bm{x}_{i}\rangle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{1+0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}}\right),

which further indicates, for large enough
t
, we have

A_{6}(t)\leq\max\left\{\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{1+0.5\min\{\mu_{-},0.5\}}}\right),\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\min\{1+\mu_{+},1.5-\varepsilon\}}}\right)\right\},

which indicates

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}A_{6}(t)<\infty.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left(g(t+1)-g(t)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{r}(t+1)-\bm{r}(t)\|^{2}+\left\langle\bm{r}(t),-\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}_{\bm{B}(t)}(\bm{w}(t))-\frac{N}{bt}\sum_{i:\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{B}(t)\cap\bm{S}_{s}}\bm{v}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{r}(t+1)-\bm{r}(t)\|^{2}+\eta A_{6}(t)+\eta A_{7}(t)\right)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\infty.

The proof is completed.
∎

B.2.3
Explanation for proper lyapunov function

Based on the success of applying Lyapunov function
{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{\beta}{2\eta}\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\|^{2}
to analyze gradient descent with momentum, it is natural to try to extend this routine to analyze stochastic gradient descent with momentum. However, in this section, we will show such Lyapunov function is not proper to analyze SGDM as this will put constraints on the range of the momentum rate
\beta
. Specifically, at any step
t
, since the loss
{\mathcal{L}}
is
\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{N}
smooth at
\bm{w}(t)
, we can expand the loss
{\mathcal{L}}
in the same way as the GDM case:

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t+1))\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\langle\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t),\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}\|\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\|^{2}.

By taking expectation with respect to
\bm{w}(t+1)
conditioning on
\{\bm{w}(s)\}_{s=1}^{t}
for both sides, we further obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t+1))\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right],\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\rangle+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\|^{2}\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(\star)}{=}

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{1}{(1-\beta)\eta}\left\langle\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right],\beta\left(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\|^{2}\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{\beta}{(1-\beta)\eta}\left\langle\left(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\right),\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\|^{2}\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]-\frac{1}{(1-\beta)\eta}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{\beta}{2(1-\beta)\eta}\left\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\right\|^{2}+\frac{\beta}{2(1-\beta)\eta}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\|^{2}\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]-\frac{1}{(1-\beta)\eta}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2},

where Eq.
(\star)
is becasue
\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]=-(1-\beta)\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\beta\left(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\right)
due to the definition of SGDM (Eq. (
2
)). Rearranging the above inequality and taking expectations of both sides with respect to
\{\bm{w}(s)\}_{s=1}^{t}
leads to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t+1))\right]+\frac{2-\beta}{2(1-\beta)\eta}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{\beta}{2(1-\beta)\eta}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\right\|^{2}.

(42)

On the other hand, we wish to obtain some positive constant
\alpha
from Eq. (
42
), such that (at least),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t+1))\right]+\alpha{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\right\|^{2}.

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\alpha{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\right\|^{2},

(43)

which requires to lower bound
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2}
by
{\mathbb{E}}

\left\|\ \bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\right\|^{2}
. However, in general cases,
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2}
is only upper bounded by
{\mathbb{E}}

\left\|\ \bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\right\|^{2}
(Holder’s Inequality), although in our case,
\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2}
can be bounded as

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|-(1-\beta)\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\beta\left(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|-(1-\beta)\eta\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}+\left\|\beta\left(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\right)\right\|^{2}+2\beta(1-\beta)\eta\langle\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1),-\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\rangle,

while by the separability of the dataset and that the loss is non-increasing,
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\ \bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\right\|^{2}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right]
can be bounded as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\ \bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\right\|^{2}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{N\sigma_{max}^{2}}{b\gamma^{2}}\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)\left|{\mathcal{F}}_{t}\right.\right]\right\|^{2}.

(44)

By Eqs. (
42
) and (
44
), we have that to ensure Eq. (
43
), it is required that

\displaystyle\frac{2-\beta}{2(1-\beta)\eta}\frac{b\gamma^{2}}{N\sigma_{max}^{2}}-\frac{H\sigma_{max}^{2}}{2N}\geq\frac{\beta}{2(1-\beta)\eta},

which puts additional constraint on
\beta
as

\beta\leq\frac{2b\gamma^{2}-H\eta\sigma_{max}^{4}}{b\gamma^{2}+N\sigma_{max}^{2}-H\sigma_{max}^{4}\eta}.

Specifically, the upper bound becomes close to
0
when
N
becomes large, and constrains
\beta
in a small range.

Appendix C
Implicit regularization of deterministic Adam

This section collects the proof of the convergent direction of Adam, i.e., Theorem
4
. The methodology of this section bears great similarity with GDM, although the preconditioner of Adam requires specific treatment for analysis. The proof is still divided into two stages: (1). we first prove the sum of squared gradients along the trajectory is finite. Additionally, we prove the convergent rate of loss is
\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{t})
; (2). we prove
\bm{w}(t)-\ln(t)\hat{\bm{w}}
has bounded norm. Before we present these two stages of proof, we will first give the required range of
\eta
for which Theorem
3
holds. The analyses of this section hold for almost every dataset, and the "almost every" constraint is further moved in Section
E.1
.

C.1
Choice of learning rate

Let
H_{s_{0}}
be the smooth parameter over
[s_{0},\infty)
given by Assumption
3
. (D). Let
\beta_{2}=(c\beta_{1})^{4}
(
c>1
). The "sufficiently small learning rate" in Theorem
3
means

\eta\leq\frac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}\inf_{t\geq 2}\left(\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}\right)}{H_{\ell^{-1}((1-c\beta_{1})^{-1}N{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)))}}.

To ensure
\eta
is well-defined, we need to prove

\inf_{t\geq 2}\left(\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}\right)>0,

and we introduce the following technical lemma:

Lemma 16
.

Define
f_{t}(x)=\frac{1-x^{t}}{x(1-x^{t-1})}
,
\forall t\in\mathbb{Z},t\geq 2
. We have
f_{t}(x)
is decreasing with respect to
x
.
Furthermore, for any
x\in[0,1)
, we have

f(x)\geq\sqrt[4]{f(x^{4})}.

(45)

Proof.

First of all, by definition,

f(x)=\frac{1-x^{t}}{x-x^{t}}=1+\frac{1-x}{x-x^{t}}=1+\frac{1-x}{x(1-x^{t-1})}=1+\frac{1}{x(1+x+\cdots+x^{t-2})}

is monotonously decreasing as
0\leq x<1
. Secondly, Eq. (
45
) is equivalent to

\displaystyle\frac{(1-x^{t})^{4}}{\beta^{4}_{1}(1-x^{t-1})^{4}}\geq\frac{(1-x^{4t})}{x^{4}(1-x^{4(t-1)})}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle\frac{(1-x^{t})^{3}}{(1-x^{t-1})^{3}}\geq\frac{(1+x^{t})(1+x^{2t})}{(1+x^{t-1})(1+x^{2(t-1)})}.

The left side of the above inequality is no smaller than 1, while the right side is no larger than 1, which completes the proof.
∎

We are now ready to prove
\eta
is well-defined. First of all, for every
t
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{1}(1-\beta_{1}^{t-1})}{1-\beta_{1}}\left(\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{\beta_{1}(1-\beta_{1}^{t-1})}-\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{(c\beta_{1})(1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1})}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(\star)}{=}

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{1}(1-\beta_{1}^{t-1})}{1-\beta_{1}}\left(f_{t}(\beta_{1})-f_{t}(c\beta_{1})\right)>0,

(46)

where Eq. (
\star
) is by Lemma
16
and
c\beta_{1}=\sqrt[4]{\beta_{2}}<1
.

On the other hand, we have

\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\left(\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}\right)=\left(1-\frac{1}{c}\right)\frac{1}{1-\beta_{1}}.

(47)

By Eq. (
46
) and Eq. (
47
), we obtain
\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}
is lower bounded by some positive constant across
t
, and
\eta
is well defined.

C.2
Sum of gradients along the trajectory is bounded

We start with the following lemma, which indicates
{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\|^{2}
is a proper Lyapunov function for Adam.

Lemma 17
.

Let all conditions in Theorem
4
hold. Then, for any
t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(t+1)+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}.

(48)

Proof.

We start with the case
t=1
. To begin with, we have
{\mathcal{L}}
is
H_{\ell^{-1}(N{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)))}
smooth around
\bm{w}(1)
. By definition
H_{x}
is non-increasing with respect to
x
, and since
\ell^{-1}
is also non-increasing, we have

H_{\ell^{-1}(N{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)))}\leq H_{\ell^{-1}(\frac{1}{1-c\beta_{1}}N{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)))},

which further indicates when
\alpha
is small enough,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1+\alpha))\overset{(\star)}{\leq}

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))+\alpha\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)),\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\rangle+\frac{L}{2}\alpha^{2}\|\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))-\alpha\left\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)),\eta\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}}\odot\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))\right\rangle+\bm{o}(\alpha^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))-\frac{1}{2\eta}\alpha^{2}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2},

where in Eq. (
\star
) we denote
L\overset{\triangle}{=}H_{\ell^{-1}(\frac{1}{1-c\beta_{1}}N{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)))}
, and the last inequality is due to
\frac{1}{2\eta}\alpha^{2}

\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}=\bm{o}(\alpha^{2})
, and
\left\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)),\eta\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}}\odot\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))\right\rangle
is positive.

Now if there exists an
\alpha\in(0,1)
, such that Eq. (
48
) fails, we denote
\alpha^{*}=\inf\{\alpha:Eq.(\ref{eq: loss_update_adam_t})~fails~for~1+\alpha\}
. We have
\alpha^{*}>0
, and the equality in Eq. (
48
) holds for
1+\alpha^{*}
. Therefore, we have for any
\alpha\in(0,\alpha^{*})
,

{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1+\alpha))\leq{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1+\alpha))+\frac{1}{2\eta}\alpha^{2}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}\leq{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)),

which by Lemma
6
leads to
{\mathcal{L}}
is
H_{\ell^{-1}(N{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)))}
smooth (thus
L
smooth) over the set
\{\bm{w}(1+\alpha):\alpha\in[0,\alpha^{*}]\}
, and

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1+\alpha^{*}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))+\alpha^{*}\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)),\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\rangle+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))-\alpha^{*}\left\langle\frac{1}{\eta}\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot\left(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\right),\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\right\rangle+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))-\alpha^{*}\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot\left(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}}\odot\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))-\alpha^{*}\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot\left(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{L}{2\sqrt{\varepsilon}}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))-(\alpha^{*})^{2}\frac{1}{\eta}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot\left(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{L}{2\sqrt{\varepsilon}}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))-(\alpha^{*})^{2}\frac{1}{2\eta}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot\left(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\right)\right\|^{2},

(49)

where the second-to-last inequality is due to
\|\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1)\|>0
(by Lemma
9
) and
\alpha^{*}>(\alpha^{*})^{2}
, while the last inequality is due to

\displaystyle\eta\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}\inf_{t\geq 2}\left(\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}\right)}{L}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}\left(\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{2}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{2}}{1-(c\beta_{1})}\right)}{L}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}\left(1+\beta_{1}-\frac{1+(c\beta_{1})}{c}\right)}{L}=\frac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}\left(1-\frac{1}{c}\right)}{L}<\frac{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}{L}.

Eq. (
49
) contradicts the fact that the equality in Eq. (
48
) holds for
1+\alpha^{*}
, which completes the proof of
t=1
.

If
t\geq 2
, following the similar routine as
t=1
, we also prove Eq. (
48
) by reduction to absurdity. If there exist
t
and
\alpha
such that Eq. (
48
) fails. Denote
t^{*}
as the smallest time such that there exists an
\alpha\in[0,1)
such that Eq. (
48
) fails for
t^{*}
and
\alpha
. By Lemma
9
,
\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\|^{2}
is positive, and strict inequality in Eq. (
48
) holds for
t
and
\alpha=0
, which by continuity leads to

1>\alpha^{*}\overset{\triangle}{=}\inf\{\alpha\in[0,1]:Eq.(\ref{eq: loss_update_adam_t})~fails~for~1+\alpha\}>0.

Then, for any
\alpha\in[0,\alpha^{*}]
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}+\alpha))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}+\alpha))+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}}}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1}}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\|^{2}.

On the other hand, for any time
2\leq s\leq t^{*}-1
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s+1))+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{s}}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(s)}\odot(\bm{w}(s+1)-\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s))+\frac{\beta_{1}(1-\beta_{1}^{s-1})}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s-1}}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(s-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(s)-\bm{w}(s-1))\right\|^{2}.

(50)

By Eq. (
47
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{s}}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}>\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{s}}{c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}=\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{s}}{c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s+1}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s}}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s+1}},

which by
\frac{(1-\beta_{1}^{s-1})}{(1-\beta_{1}^{s})}
further leads to

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s))+\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{s-1}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s-1}}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(s-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(s)-\bm{w}(s-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s+1))+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{s}}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(s)}\odot(\bm{w}(s+1)-\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s+1))+\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{s}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s+1}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s}}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s+1}}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(s)}\odot(\bm{w}(s+1)-\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s+1}}\left({\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s+1))+\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{s}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s+1}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{s}}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(s)}\odot(\bm{w}(s+1)-\bm{w}(s))\right\|^{2}\right).

(51)

On the other hand, for
s=1
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))\geq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(2))+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\beta_{1}}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{2}}\left({\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(2))+\frac{1-\beta_{1}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{2}}{1-(c\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1))\right\|^{2}\right).

(52)

Combining Eqs. (
50
), (
51
), and (
52
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}+\alpha))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1}}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{1-(c\beta_{2})^{t^{*}-1}}\left({\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))+\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-2}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-2}}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-2)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}-1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}-2))\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\cdots

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{2}}\left({\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(2))+\frac{1-\beta_{1}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{2}}{1-(c\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(1)}\odot(\bm{w}(2)-\bm{w}(1))\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{1-c\beta_{1}}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1))<\frac{1}{1-c\beta_{1}}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)).

Therefore, by Lemma
6
,
{\mathcal{L}}
is
H_{\ell^{-1}(\frac{1}{1-c\beta_{1}}N{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(1)))}
smooth (thus
L
smooth) over the set
\{\bm{w}(t^{*}+\alpha):\alpha\in[0,\alpha^{*}]\}
, which further leads to

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}+\alpha^{*}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\alpha^{*}\langle\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*})),\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*})\rangle+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(\bullet)}{=}

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha^{*}}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\langle\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}),(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right.

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\left.\beta_{1}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*})\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha^{*}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\frac{\alpha^{*}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\langle\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}),\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*})\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha^{*}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\frac{\alpha^{*}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\langle\frac{\sqrt[8]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}}{\sqrt[8]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}}\odot\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*})),\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\left.\frac{\sqrt[8]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}}{\sqrt[8]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}}\odot\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*})\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha^{*}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\frac{(\alpha^{*})^{2}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\frac{\sqrt[8]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}}{\sqrt[8]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}}\odot\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\frac{(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\frac{\sqrt[8]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}}{\sqrt[8]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}}\odot\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(\diamond)}{\leq}

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\frac{L}{2}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*})\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha^{*}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\frac{(\alpha^{*})^{2}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{c\beta_{1}(1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\frac{(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{c\beta_{1}(1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))+\frac{L}{2\sqrt{\varepsilon}}(\alpha^{*})^{2}\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{*}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})}{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\frac{(\alpha^{*})^{2}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{c\beta_{1}(1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\frac{(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{c\beta_{1}(1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(\square)}{<}

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))-\frac{(\alpha^{*})^{2}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{c(1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))\right\|^{2},

where Eq.
(\bullet)
is due to an alternative form of the Adam’s update rule:

\displaystyle(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}})\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))-\beta_{1}(1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1})\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*})-\bm{w}(t^{*}-1))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\eta(1-\beta_{1})\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*})),

(53)

Inequality
(\diamond)
is due to

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}}{\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}}=\sqrt[4]{\frac{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}}=\sqrt[4]{\frac{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\frac{\beta_{2}\bm{\nu}(t^{*}-1)+(1-\beta_{2})\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t^{*}))^{2}}{1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt[4]{\frac{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\frac{\beta_{2}\bm{\nu}(t^{*}-1)}{1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}}}}}=\sqrt[4]{\frac{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\frac{\beta_{2}(1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}-1})\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}{1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}}}}}\leq\sqrt[4]{\frac{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}{\frac{\beta_{2}(1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}-1})\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}{1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}}}\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\frac{\beta_{2}(1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}-1})\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*}-1)}{1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt[4]{\frac{1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}}}{\beta_{2}(1-\beta_{2}^{t^{*}-1})}}\mathds{1}_{d}~(all~the~computings~are~component\text{-}wisely),

and
f(c\beta_{1})\geq\sqrt[4]{f((c\beta_{1})^{4})}
, and Inequality
(\square)
is due to

\frac{L}{2\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\leq\frac{\inf_{t\geq 2}\left(\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}\right)}{2\eta}\leq\frac{\left(\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}}}{1-\beta_{1}}-\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t^{*}-1}}{c(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t^{*}-1}}\right)}{2\eta},

\alpha^{*}>(\alpha^{*})^{2}
, and
\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t^{*})}\odot(\bm{w}(t^{*}+1)-\bm{w}(t^{*}))\right\|^{2}>0
.

This contradicts to that the equality in Eq. (
48
) holds for
t^{*}+\alpha^{*}
.

The proof is completed.
∎

As
\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\beta_{1}^{t}=0
and
\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}(c\beta_{1})^{t}=0
, we have the following corollary based on Lemma
1
.

Corollary 7
.

Let all assumptions in Theorem
4
hold. Then, for large enough
t
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t+1))+\frac{1}{2\sqrt[4]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{1}{2\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}.

(54)

Consequently, we have

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}<\infty.

(55)

The proof of Corollary
7
relies on the following classical lemma on the equivalence between the convergence of two non-negative sequence. The proof is omitted here and can be found in
[
38
]
.

Lemma 18

(c.f. Lemma 27,
[
38
]
)
.

Let
\{a_{i}\}_{i=1}^{\infty}
be a series of non-negative reals, and
\varepsilon
be a positive real. Then,
\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}a_{i}<\infty
is equivalent to
\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\frac{a_{i}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon+\sum_{s=1}^{i}a_{s}}}<\infty
.

Proof of Corollary
7
.

We have

\displaystyle\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t-1}}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\frac{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t}}{1-(c\beta_{1})^{t-1}}=\frac{1}{2c\eta(1-\beta_{1})}<\frac{1}{2\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})},

\displaystyle\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}=\frac{1}{2\eta(1-\beta_{1})}>\frac{1}{2\sqrt[4]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})},

which completes the proof of Eq. (
54
). Rearranging Eq. (
54
) leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt[4]{c}-1}{2\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt[4]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))-\left({\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t+1))+\frac{1}{2\sqrt[4]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}\right),

which by iteration further leads to that for a large enough time
T_{1}

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{1}}^{T_{2}}\frac{\sqrt[4]{c}-1}{2\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(T_{1}))+\frac{1}{2\sqrt[4]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(T_{1}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(T_{1})-\bm{w}(T_{1}-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(T_{2}+1))+\frac{1}{2\sqrt[4]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(T_{2})}\odot(\bm{w}(T_{2}+1)-\bm{w}(T_{2}+1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(T_{1}))+\frac{1}{2\sqrt[4]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(T_{1}-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(T_{1})-\bm{w}(T_{1}-1))\right\|^{2}.

Consequently, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\sqrt[4]{c}-1}{2\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}<\infty.

(56)

On the other hand, for any
t
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|\left\|{\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}}\odot\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left\langle\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)),\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle=\left\langle\sqrt[2]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t)),\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle-\eta\hat{\bm{m}}(t),\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle=-\frac{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}\left\langle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\beta_{1}^{t-s}\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s)),\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}\frac{1}{N}\left\langle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\beta_{1}^{t-s}\sum_{\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{x}_{i},\bm{w}(s)\rangle)\bm{x}_{i},\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle\geq-\frac{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\beta_{1}^{t-s}\sum_{\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{x}_{i},\bm{w}(s)\rangle)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bm{x}_{i}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{x}_{i},\bm{w}(t)\rangle)\geq\frac{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|,

which by Eq. (
56
) indicates

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}\right)^{2}\frac{\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}{\left\|{\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}}\odot\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|^{2}}<\infty.

As
\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\left(\frac{\eta(1-\beta_{1})}{1-\beta_{1}^{t}}\right)^{2}=\eta^{2}(1-\beta_{1})^{2}
, we then obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}{{\sqrt[2]{\varepsilon+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}}}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}{{\sqrt[2]{\varepsilon+\sum_{s=1}^{t}(1-\beta)\beta^{t-s}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{1-\beta}}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}{{\sqrt[2]{\varepsilon+\frac{\sum_{s=1}^{t}(1-\beta)\beta^{t-s}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}{1-\beta^{t}}}}}\leq d\sqrt{\frac{1}{1-\beta}}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}{\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\frac{\sum_{s=1}^{t}(1-\beta)\beta^{t-s}\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))^{2}}{1-\beta^{t}}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle d\sqrt{\frac{1}{1-\beta}}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}{\left\|{\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}}\right\|^{2}}\leq d\|\hat{\bm{w}}\|^{2}_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{1}{1-\beta}}\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}}{\left\|{\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}}\odot\hat{\bm{w}}\right\|^{2}}<\infty,

which by Lemma
18
completes the proof.
∎

Based on Corollary
7
, we can further prove Lemma
19
, characterizing the convergent rate of loss
{\mathcal{L}}
directly.

Lemma 19
.

Let all conditions in Theorem
4
hold. Then,

{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))=\Theta\left(t^{-1}\right),\|\bm{w}(t)\|=\Theta(\ln(t))
,
and
\|\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1)\|=\Theta(t^{-1})
.

Proof of Lemma
19
.

To begin with, Eq. (
53
) indicates

\displaystyle\|\eta(1-\beta_{1})\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|(1-\beta_{1}^{t})\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))-\beta_{1}(1-\beta_{1}^{t-1})\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|(1-\beta_{1}^{t})\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|+\left\|\beta_{1}(1-\beta_{1}^{t-1})\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|+\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left(\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}+\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}\right)

(57)

On the other hand, by Corollary
7
,

\sum_{s=1}^{\infty}\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(s))\|^{2}<\infty,

which following the same routine as Corollary
6
leads to

\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle\rightarrow\infty,\forall\bm{x}\in\tilde{\bm{S}}.

Therefore, by Lemma
7
, there exists a large enough time
T_{1}
, such that
\forall t\geq T_{1}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\ell(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle)\leq-\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle)\leq K\ell(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle),\forall\bm{x}\in\bm{S},

which by the separable assumption further leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{K}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\leq-\frac{\gamma}{N}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle)\leq\frac{1}{N}\left\langle-\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle)\bm{x},\gamma\hat{\bm{w}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left\|\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle)\bm{x}\right\|\|\gamma\hat{\bm{w}}\|=\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{\bm{x}\in\bm{S}}\ell^{\prime}(\langle\bm{w}(t),\bm{x}\rangle)\leq K{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t)).

(58)

Combining Eq. (
26
) and the above inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\eta(1-\beta_{1})\gamma}{K}\right)^{2}{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))^{2}\leq 2\left(\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~`~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~+\left.\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}\right).

(59)

On the other hand, by Eq. (
56
), we have

\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}<\infty.

Therefore, there exists large enough time
T_{2}
, such that
\forall t>T_{2}
,

\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}<1,

and thus,

\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{4}<\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}.

(60)

Combining Eq. (
59
) and Eq. (
60
), there exists a positive real constant
C
, such that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))^{2}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{4}\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\left\|\sqrt{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}\right).

Rearranging Eq. (
54
) leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt[4]{c}-1}{4\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left(\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~+\left.\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{\sqrt[4]{c}+1}{4\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~-\left({\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t+1))+\frac{\sqrt[4]{c}+1}{4\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t)}\odot(\bm{w}(t+1)-\bm{w}(t))\right\|^{2}\right),

which further indicates

\displaystyle\left({\mathcal{L}}(\bm{w}(t))+\frac{\sqrt[4]{c}+1}{4\sqrt[2]{c}\eta(1-\beta_{1})}\left\|\sqrt[4]{\varepsilon\mathds{1}_{d}+\hat{\bm{\nu}}(t-1)}\odot(\bm{w}(t)-\bm{w}(t-1))\right\|^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq
